# digitRecognition.dice, translated by tddlog's scripts/dice2alea.py.
module AleaProgram
using Alea
# Julia would spend more time optimizing the long straight-line functions
# below than running them takes: they are interpreted, and Alea's own
# methods compile as usual.
Base.Experimental.@compiler_options compile=min optimize=0 infer=false

const EV = Ref{AnyBool}(true)
observe!(path, c) = (EV[] = EV[] & (!path | c); true)

function shr(x::DistUInt{W}, n::Integer) where W
    k = min(n, W)
    DistUInt{W}(vcat(fill(false, k), x.bits[1:W - k]))
end

components(x::Tuple) = reduce(vcat, map(components, x); init = Any[])
components(x) = Any[x]
width(::DistUInt{W}) where W = W

function warmup()
    a = flip(0.7)
    x = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    y = uniform(DistUInt{3}, 1, 6)
    z = ifelse(a, x + DistUInt{2}(1), x - DistUInt{2}(1))
    t = ifelse(flip(0.4), (a, z), (!a, x * x))
    e = prob_equals(z, DistUInt{2}(2)) | (x < z) | xor(a, flip(0.2))
    pr(flip(0.7))
    pr(a, z, y, t...; evidence = e)
    nothing
end

function dice_main()
    x1_y = discrete(DistUInt{4}, [0.09871660513357945, 0.1123695505217979, 0.09929960280158878, 0.10217959128163487, 0.09736661053355784, 0.09034963860144558, 0.09863260546957811, 0.1044195823216707, 0.09751660993356026, 0.09914960340158638])
    x2_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(0))
    if x2_result !== false
     chunk_1(x2_result)
     chunk_2(x2_result)
     chunk_3(x2_result)
     chunk_4(x2_result)
    end
    if x2_result !== true
     x786_path = !(x2_result)
     x787_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(1))
     if x787_result !== false
      (x788_path, x988_obs983,) = chunk_5(x786_path, x787_result)
      x1188_obs1183 = chunk_6(x788_path, x988_obs983)
      x1388_obs1383 = chunk_7(x788_path, x1188_obs1183)
      chunk_8(x788_path, x1388_obs1383)
     end
     if x787_result !== true
      x1572_path = (x786_path & !(x787_result))
      x1573_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(2))
      if x1573_result !== false
       (x1574_path, x1774_obs1766,) = chunk_9(x1572_path, x1573_result)
       x1974_obs1966 = chunk_10(x1574_path, x1774_obs1766)
       x2174_obs2166 = chunk_11(x1574_path, x1974_obs1966)
       chunk_12(x1574_path, x2174_obs2166)
      end
      if x1573_result !== true
       x2358_path = (x1572_path & !(x1573_result))
       x2359_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(3))
       if x2359_result !== false
        (x2360_path, x2560_obs2549,) = chunk_13(x2358_path, x2359_result)
        x2760_obs2749 = chunk_14(x2360_path, x2560_obs2549)
        x2960_obs2949 = chunk_15(x2360_path, x2760_obs2749)
        chunk_16(x2360_path, x2960_obs2949)
       end
       if x2359_result !== true
        x3144_path = (x2358_path & !(x2359_result))
        x3145_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(4))
        if x3145_result !== false
         (x3146_path, x3346_obs3332,) = chunk_17(x3144_path, x3145_result)
         x3546_obs3532 = chunk_18(x3146_path, x3346_obs3332)
         x3746_obs3732 = chunk_19(x3146_path, x3546_obs3532)
         chunk_20(x3146_path, x3746_obs3732)
        end
        if x3145_result !== true
         x3930_path = (x3144_path & !(x3145_result))
         x3931_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(5))
         if x3931_result !== false
          (x3932_path, x4132_obs4115,) = chunk_21(x3930_path, x3931_result)
          x4332_obs4315 = chunk_22(x3932_path, x4132_obs4115)
          x4532_obs4515 = chunk_23(x3932_path, x4332_obs4315)
          chunk_24(x3932_path, x4532_obs4515)
         end
         if x3931_result !== true
          x4716_path = (x3930_path & !(x3931_result))
          x4717_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(6))
          if x4717_result !== false
           (x4718_path, x4918_obs4898,) = chunk_25(x4716_path, x4717_result)
           x5118_obs5098 = chunk_26(x4718_path, x4918_obs4898)
           x5318_obs5298 = chunk_27(x4718_path, x5118_obs5098)
           chunk_28(x4718_path, x5318_obs5298)
          end
          if x4717_result !== true
           x5502_path = (x4716_path & !(x4717_result))
           x5503_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(7))
           if x5503_result !== false
            (x5504_path, x5704_obs5681,) = chunk_29(x5502_path, x5503_result)
            x5904_obs5881 = chunk_30(x5504_path, x5704_obs5681)
            x6104_obs6081 = chunk_31(x5504_path, x5904_obs5881)
            chunk_32(x5504_path, x6104_obs6081)
           end
           if x5503_result !== true
            x6288_path = (x5502_path & !(x5503_result))
            x6289_result = prob_equals(x1_y, DistUInt{4}(8))
            if x6289_result !== false
             (x6290_path, x6490_obs6464,) = chunk_33(x6288_path, x6289_result)
             x6690_obs6664 = chunk_34(x6290_path, x6490_obs6464)
             x6890_obs6864 = chunk_35(x6290_path, x6690_obs6664)
             chunk_36(x6290_path, x6890_obs6864)
            end
            if x6289_result !== true
             (x7074_path, x7274_obs7247,) = chunk_37(x6288_path, x6289_result)
             x7474_obs7447 = chunk_38(x7074_path, x7274_obs7247)
             x7674_obs7647 = chunk_39(x7074_path, x7474_obs7447)
             chunk_40(x7074_path, x7674_obs7647)
            end
            x7858_result = x6289_result === true ? x1_y : x6289_result === false ? x1_y : ifelse(x6289_result, x1_y, x1_y)
           end
           x7859_result = x5503_result === true ? x1_y : x5503_result === false ? x7858_result : ifelse(x5503_result, x1_y, x7858_result)
          end
          x7860_result = x4717_result === true ? x1_y : x4717_result === false ? x7859_result : ifelse(x4717_result, x1_y, x7859_result)
         end
         x7861_result = x3931_result === true ? x1_y : x3931_result === false ? x7860_result : ifelse(x3931_result, x1_y, x7860_result)
        end
        x7862_result = x3145_result === true ? x1_y : x3145_result === false ? x7861_result : ifelse(x3145_result, x1_y, x7861_result)
       end
       x7863_result = x2359_result === true ? x1_y : x2359_result === false ? x7862_result : ifelse(x2359_result, x1_y, x7862_result)
      end
      x7864_result = x1573_result === true ? x1_y : x1573_result === false ? x7863_result : ifelse(x1573_result, x1_y, x7863_result)
     end
     x7865_result = x787_result === true ? x1_y : x787_result === false ? x7864_result : ifelse(x787_result, x1_y, x7864_result)
    end
    x7866_result = x2_result === true ? x1_y : x2_result === false ? x7865_result : ifelse(x2_result, x1_y, x7865_result)
    return x7866_result
end

function report(k, x, d)
    if x isa AnyBool
        println("alea ", k, " true ", d[true])
        println("alea ", k, " false ", d[false])
    else
        for v in 0:(2^width(x) - 1)
            println("alea ", k, " ", v, " ", d[v])
        end
    end
end

function run_program()
    EV[] = true
    stats = @timed begin
        comps = components(dice_main())
        dists = pr(comps...; evidence = EV[])
        (comps, length(comps) == 1 ? [dists] : dists)
    end
    comps, dists = stats.value
    for k in eachindex(comps)
        report(k, comps[k], dists[k])
    end
    println("alea_seconds ", stats.time)
    println("alea_compile_seconds ", get(stats, :compile_time, NaN))
end

warmup()
task = Task(run_program, 1 << 30)
schedule(task)
wait(task)
end
